# OptionValueJEPA 0DTE Value And 5m Exit

Candidate labels: `research_papers\JEPA\results\jepa_full_pipeline_option_policy\candidate_labels.parquet`
Train cutoff: `20260331`
Test start: `20260401`
Dynamic target: `future_edge`
Max hold: `180`m, hard stop `-60%`, learned exit min hold `15`m.

## Interpretation

- `fixed_delta_0.70_hard` is the current deployable reference from prior validation.
- `option_value_*_hard` tests whether OptionValueJEPA can choose a better delta/strike while keeping the mechanical exit.
- `option_value_*_learned_exit` tests the same strike selector plus a continuation-value exit evaluated every 5m.
- `*_trail_cutoff` uses the configured trailing-stop exit and entry cutoff; the OptionValue selector is selected only on validation months.
- `*_learned_profit_lock` uses the dynamic JEPA continuation head only after the trade is already profitable, optionally gating the mechanical trail with predicted future edge.
- Oracle rows are non-deployable ceilings.

## Validation

- Exit margin selected on validation: `0.0300`.
- Trail selector selected on validation: `{'score_base': 'ovjepa_pred_best', 'delta_bonus': 0.0, 'min_delta_abs': 0.6, 'score': 261.38835706544097}`.
- Learned profit-lock selected on validation: `{'margin': 0.03, 'profit_activation_pct': 1.5, 'trail_activation_pct': None, 'trail_drawdown_pct': None, 'score': 306.5183727492521}`.
- Blended selector tested: `{'score_base': 'ovjepa_pred_rule_best_mean', 'delta_bonus': 2.0, 'min_delta_abs': None}`.
